That scope retrospectively activates root `ر ح م`, `B003`, womb as the child's growing vessel, and `B002`, lines of descent and kinship, in the doubled opening adjectives. The womb branch supplies embodied generation, the kinship branch social continuity, and `ٱلْأَبْتَرُ` their negation. The packet contains no explicit child or family noun, so I infer this relation solely from the accepted branches' shared continuity domain and the opening/closing frame. Alongside loss of mention and good, the final predicate can therefore retain a distinct genealogical reading sharpened by the mercy root.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_27a023bde88bbc1b3d29","text":"No later ayah follows 108:3, so no later surprise emerged after it. The retrospective change came from earlier material now concentrated in the closing: س م و B005/B008 and ك ث ر B001/B002 made “abtar” a loss of public continuation; ر ح م B002/B003 made it a loss of relational/womb continuity; and ن ح ر B002 made it the sterile counterpart to sacrificial cutting. The closing became denser after rereading because each prior ayah supplied a different kind of continuity that the hater lacks.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B004","root_000820/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_343bf6c8bea4f51399ef","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:3:2\",\"root_000820\",[\"B002\"]],[\"108:3:4\",\"root_000080\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kin, iğrenerek geri çekilme ve ilişkiden uzaklaşma hareketine dönüşür; bu uzaklaşma, kin duyanın kendisini akrabalık ve merhamet bağından koparır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B001","root_000080/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_40893951d129353ae495","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:3:4\",\"root_000080\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Son kesiklik, ibadet içindeki kurban kesiminin tersine, hiçbir sunu, iyilik veya devam üretmeyen kısır bir son olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B002","root_000080/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_477112b6790932d5de25","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:3:4\",\"root_000080\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Soyun kesilmesi, açılıştaki koruyucu akrabalık alanının karşıtı olarak üretken ve ilişkisel sürekliliğin yitirilmesine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B002","root_000820/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_489d98d289d815c9fd17","text":"1. **The hostile judgement reverses onto its bearer.** Fixed `شَانِئَكَ`, root `ش ن ء`, `B001`, supplies hatred, enmity, and the hater; fixed `ٱلْأَبْتَرُ`, root `ب ت ر`, `B002`, supplies loss of posterity, mention, or good effect. The second-person object marks the target of hostility, but emphatic `هُوَ` and the definite predicate assign cutoff to the hater himself. The first root supplies the hostile relation and the second its reversed outcome. The packet does not state what deprivation the hater intended; I infer only that hostility seeks the target's diminishment and that the syntax relocates diminishment onto its source. After the preceding gift of abundance, the clause reads as a reversal of hostile judgement rather than a free-standing insult.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000820/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_51cc7e3f1498703652ae","text":"2. **Aversive distancing becomes self-severance.** Dormant fixed `ش ن ء`, `B002`, supplies disgusted recoil and distancing, while fixed `ب ت ر`, `B004`, supplies severing kinship. The opening root `ر ح م`, `B002`, has already established kinship and maintained bond as the counter-domain. Within the fixed clause, aversion functions as the separating action and severance as the relational result. The packet supplies all three branch scopes, but the directional arrow recoil -> broken bond is my abductive move; grammar predicates only the resulting condition. The ayah can therefore diagnose a mechanism: the hater's own withdrawal from relation is what leaves him cut off.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B001","root_000820/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_89537c99f6b1e4695583","text":"3. **The hater is an unsuccessful claimant who must release what is not his.** Lexical evidence: ش ن ء B003, acknowledging and releasing a right; ع ط و B002, handing over and giving; ر ب ب B001, ownership and authority; ب ت ر B001/B002, cut short and cut off from good. Structural cue: the packet’s grant is explicitly from “we” to “you,” and the command directs the response to “your Rabb”; the hater appears only after this transfer and orientation have been established. Assigned role: sh-n-ʾ B003 supplies a latent legal/possessive action of conceding or giving out a right; Rabb supplies rightful ownership; the gift supplies authorized transfer. Abductive move: I infer that the enemy’s hostility masks a failed claim over honor, abundance, or authority, and that the established giver-owner relationship forces that claim out of his hand. Reading change: “shāniʾaka” can be heard not only as one who hates but as a dispossessed opposer whose stance results in concession and loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_909c95c777a69d12f399","text":"4. **The final cut is the sterile opposite of sacrificial cutting.** Lexical evidence: ب ت ر B001, cutting something short before completion; ن ح ر B002, sacrificial slaughter; ن ح ر B004, mutual contention as throat-to-throat attack; ص ل و B003, prescribed worship. Structural cue: a commanded, God-directed nahr comes immediately before a hostile figure defined by abtar. Assigned role: nahr provides a ritually ordered cut; abtar provides a premature, fruitless cut; sh-n-ʾ provides the hostile will that belongs to the second kind. Abductive move: I infer a contrast between cutting that participates in worship and cutting that ends in severance. Reading change: “abtar” reads as the enemy’s failed anti-sacrifice: he is not merely defeated, but left with a cut that produces no worship, no gift, and no continuation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000820/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_94f361c8bc041a5fbc3f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:3:2\",\"root_000820\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kin duyan, kendisine ait olmayan onur ve bolluk üzerindeki iddiasını bırakmak zorunda kalan başarısız bir iddiacı olarak da okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B002","root_000820/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a0adf0bf23dc3936e7ea","text":"1. **The hater is cut off in the same public field where the addressee is raised.** Lexical evidence: ش ن ء B001, hatred and enmity; ب ت ر B002, cut-off progeny, renown, and good; س م و B005/B008, name as raised designation and good repute; ك ث ر B001/B002, abundance and outnumbering rivalry. Structural cue: the surah begins with raised naming and then gives “kawthar,” but ends by restricting “abtar” to “your hater” with هو. Assigned role: the hater supplies hostility; abtar supplies loss of continuation; s-m-w and k-th-r supply the counter-fields of elevated mention and abundant continuation. Abductive move: I infer that the hater’s attempt to damage the addressee’s name rebounds because the packet has already installed name-elevation and abundance. Reading change: the final ayah reads not as a bare insult but as a reversal of social erasure: the one who hates becomes the one whose trace is severed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c1beb039e97bdec71987","text":"3. **A life or project arrested before completion.** Fixed `ٱلْأَبْتَرُ` activates root `ب ت ر`, `B001`, cutting something short before it reaches completion. The immediately prior `رَبِّكَ` activates the opposed root `ر ب ب`, `B002`, nurture, repair, and completion stage by stage; gift, prayer, and sacrifice provide the sequence's positive developing course. `B001` assigns the predicate the role of interrupted process rather than mere social status. The packet supplies completion and premature cutting; I infer that their adjacency contrasts two trajectories, one being carried onward and one failing to mature. `ٱلْأَبْتَرُ` thus reads as structurally unfinished, not only deprived of a particular possession.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B004","root_000820/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c72a1696566c2a751736","text":"2. **Hatred becomes aversive distancing that severs kinship.** Lexical evidence: ش ن ء B002, disgusted avoidance and recoiling from filth; ب ت ر B004, severing kinship; ر ح م B002/B003, kinship bond and womb. Structural cue: doubled ر ح م at the opening is answered by a final figure whose root-pair can describe disgusted distance plus kinship severance. Assigned role: sh-n-ʾ B002 explains the emotional/spatial recoil; b-t-r B004 supplies the social cut; r-h-m supplies the bond or generative enclosure being refused. Abductive move: I infer that hatred cuts the hater out of the mercy/kinship field, not merely out of numerical posterity. Reading change: “abtar” becomes relational exile: the hater is one who has made himself unable to remain in the womb/kinship circuit of mercy.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dfa706ea237baff47ff5","text":"No later ayah exists in the clipped focus window, so no later-ayah surprise emerged for `108:3`. The whole-output reread did sharpen backward relations to the opening name and mercy roots and to the prior completing sense of `رَبِّكَ`, but those are earlier contextual activators rather than later ones.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f4397a76a7f84ead7863","text":"4. **Cut-off renown closes a frame opened by naming.** The mention-and-good component of fixed `ب ت ر`, `B002`, is activated by opening `بِسْمِ`: root `س م و`, `B005`, name as raised designation, together with `B001` elevated mention and `B008` spreading good repute. `ٱلْكَوْثَرَ` (`ك ث ر`, `B001`) adds the opposite pressure of increase. The final predicate's role is to terminate hostile mention and effect, while the opening name and middle abundance establish designation and proliferation. The packet supplies this opening-to-closing arrangement; I infer that public and temporal remembrance is the field on which the contrast operates. The last word can therefore mean that hostility lacks a continuing aftereffect, while named beneficence occupies the durable frame.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000080/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f5be63106369e5411373","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"108:3:4\",\"root_000080\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kesiklik, soyla sınırlı kalmaz; kin duyanın toplumdaki anılmasını, iyi etkisini ve kalıcı izini de kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
